    LIMIT @p

    SELECT "d"."Id", "d"."ContentType", "d"."FileName", "d"."FilePath", "d"."RelatedCompoundId", "d"."Title", "d"."UploadedAt", "d"."UploadedById", "c"."Id" AS "Id0", "c"."CategoryId", "c"."CreatedAt", "c"."CreatedById", "c"."Description", "c"."MolecularFormula", "c"."Name", "c"."ReviewComments", "c"."Status", "c"."Synonym", "c"."UpdatedAt", "a"."Id" AS "Id1", "a"."AccessFailedCount", "a"."ConcurrencyStamp", "a"."CreatedAt" AS "CreatedAt0", "a"."Email", "a"."EmailConfirmed", "a"."FullName", "a"."IsActive", "a"."LockoutEnabled", "a"."LockoutEnd", "a"."NormalizedEmail", "a"."NormalizedUserName", "a"."PasswordHash", "a"."PhoneNumber", "a"."PhoneNumberConfirmed", "a"."SecurityStamp", "a"."TwoFactorEnabled", "a"."UserName"
    FROM "Documents" AS "d"
    LEFT JOIN "Compounds" AS "c" ON "d"."RelatedCompoundId" = "c"."Id"
    INNER JOIN "AspNetUsers" AS "a" ON "d"."UploadedById" = "a"."Id"
    WHERE "d"."Id" = @id
    LIMIT 1
) AS "s"
LEFT JOIN "DocumentChunks" AS "d0" ON "s"."Id" = "d0"."DocumentId"
ORDER BY "s"."Id", "s"."Id0", "s"."Id1"
